> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-ix"></a>
> > **(ix)**  the company’s constitution, or contracts with members, contains such other provisions as are prescribed by the regulations (if any), being provisions necessary to ensure that the interests of members or their agents are protected adequately;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-x"></a>
> > **(x)**  the company’s constitution requires the company to <a id="s995-1-hold"></a>hold amounts on trust for copyright owners who are not members, or for members pending the payment of amounts to them;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-xi"></a>
> > **(xi)**  the company’s constitution, or contracts with members, allows all members to access the company’s records;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-xii"></a>
> > **(xii)**  other conditions prescribed by the regulations (if any) <a id="s995-1-for-the-purposes-of-this-subparagraph-are-met-core-rd-activities"></a>for the purposes of this subparagraph are met. <a id="s995-1-core-rd-activities"></a>core <a id="s995-1-rd-activities"></a>R&D activities has the meaning given by section 355-25. <a id="s995-1-core-shipping-activities"></a>core <a id="s995-1-shipping-activities"></a>shipping activities has the meaning given by section 51-110. <a id="s995-1-corporate-change"></a>corporate change has the meaning given by section 166-175. corporate collective investment vehicle or CCIV has the same meaning as in the Corporations Act 2001. corporate limited partnership has the meaning given by section 94D of the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936. Income Tax Assessment Act 1997                            205 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 corporate tax entity has the meaning given by section 960-115. <a id="s995-1-corporate-tax-gross-up-rate-of-an-entity-for-an-income-year-means-the-amount-worked-out-using-the-following-formula"></a>corporate tax gross-up rate, of an entity for an income year, means <a id="s995-1-the-amount-worked-out-using-the-following-formula"></a>the amount worked out using the following formula: * 100% - <a id="s995-1-corporate-tax-rate"></a><a id="s995-1-corporate-tax-rate"></a>corporate tax rate for imputation purposes of the entity for the income year *Corporate tax rate for imputation purposes of the entity for the income year corporate tax rate:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  in relation to a company to which paragraph 23(2)(a) of the Income Tax Rates Act 1986 applies—means the rate of tax in respect of the taxable income of a company covered by that paragraph; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  in relation to another entity—means the rate of tax in respect of the taxable income of a company covered by <a id="s995-1-paragraph-232b-of-that-act-corporate-tax-rate-for-imputation-purposes-of-an-entity-for-an-income-year-means"></a>paragraph 23(2)(b) of that Act. corporate tax rate for imputation purposes, of an entity for an income year, means:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-i"></a>
> > **(i)**  the entity’s *aggregated turnover for the income year is equal to its aggregated turnover for the previous income year; and

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-ii"></a>
> > **(ii)**  the entity’s base rate entity passive income (within the meaning of the Income Tax Rates Act 1986) for the income year is equal to its base rate entity passive income for the previous income year; and

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-iii"></a>
> > **(iii)**  the entity’s assessable income for the income year is equal to its assessable income for the previous income year; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>

Income Tax Assessment Act 1997                            211 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 <a id="s995-1-defence-minister"></a>Defence Minister means the Minister administering section 1 of the Defence Act 1903. <a id="s995-1-defence-secretary"></a>Defence Secretary means the Secretary of the Department administered by the *Defence Minister. deferral reversal, for a *superannuation interest, has the meaning given by section 133-25 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953. <a id="s995-1-deferred-bas-payer-at-a-particular-time-means-an-entity-that-has-an-obligation-to-notify-the-commissioner-of-a-bas-amount-at-that-time-other-than"></a>deferred BAS payer, at a particular time, means an entity that has an obligation to notify the Commissioner of a *BAS amount at that <a id="s995-1-time-other-than"></a>time, other than:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  as a result of the roll-over all or part of a *capital gain from the disposal or CGT event was disregarded. The amount of the deferred roll-over gain is equal to the amount of the capital gain that was disregarded, reduced by the amount (if any) by which the gain has been taken into account in working out a *<a id="s995-1-net-capital-gain"></a>net capital gain (section 102-5) or *<a id="s995-1-net-capital-loss"></a>net capital loss (section 102-10) in relation to the asset between the roll-over time and the particular time. 212                                  Income Tax Assessment Act 1997 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 The Dictionary Chapter 6 Dictionary definitions Part 6-5 Definitions Division 995 Section 995-1 <a id="s995-1-deferred-roll-over-loss"></a><a id="s995-1-deferred-roll-over-loss"></a>deferred roll-over loss: an asset has a deferred roll-over loss at a particular time if:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  before that time there was a roll-over under a provision or former provision of this Act in relation to a disposal or a *CGT event that happened in relation to the asset; and

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  as a result of the roll-over all or part of a *capital loss from the disposal or CGT event was disregarded. The amount of the deferred roll-over loss is equal to the amount of the capital loss that was disregarded, reduced by the amount (if any) by which the loss has been taken into account in working out a *net capital gain (section 102-5) or *net capital loss (section 102-10) in relation to the asset between the roll-over time and the particular time. <a id="s995-1-deferred-superannuation-income-stream"></a>deferred superannuation income stream has the meaning given by the Superannuation Industry (Supervision) Regulations 1994. deferred to a debt account, for a *superannuation interest, in relation to *assessed Division 293 tax, has the meaning given by section 133-10 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953. deferred to a <a id="s995-1-division-296-debt-account"></a>Division 296 debt account, for a *superannuation interest, in relation to *assessed Division 296 tax, has the meaning <a id="s995-1-given-by-section-134-10-in-schedule-1-to-the-taxation-administration-act-1953-deficit"></a>given by section 134-10 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953. deficit:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  in relation to a *film, means expenditure to the extent to which it is incurred in meeting the development costs for the <a id="s995-1-film-and-includes-expenditure-to-the-extent-to-which-it-is-incurred-on-any-of-the-following"></a>film and includes expenditure to the extent to which it is <a id="s995-1-incurred-on-any-of-the-following"></a>incurred on any of the following:

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-i"></a>
> > **(i)**  location surveys and other activities undertaken to assess locations for possible use in the film;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-ii"></a>
> > **(ii)**  storyboarding for the film;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-iii"></a>
> > **(iii)**  scriptwriting for the film;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-iv"></a>
> > **(iv)**  research for the film;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-v"></a>
> > **(v)**  casting actors for the film;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-vi"></a>
> > **(vi)**  developing a budget for the film; Income Tax Assessment Act 1997                            215 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026

> > <a id="s995-1-1-a-vii"></a>

For an entity that adopts an accounting period in place of the <a id="s995-1-particular-income-year-the-reference-includes"></a>particular income year, the reference includes:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-i"></a>

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-ii"></a>
> > **(ii)**  in relation to the ending of the income year—the accounting period ending under that section on the day on which the adopted accounting period would (but for that section) have ended.

> **Note:** The Commissioner can allow you to adopt an accounting period ending on a day other than 30 June. See section 18 of the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936.

> **Note:** An accounting period ends, and a new accounting period starts, when a partnership becomes, or ceases to be, a VCLP, an ESVCLP, an AFOF or a VCMP. See section 18A of the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936. > <a id="s995-1-1-d"></a>
> **(d)**  of an AMIT—means the AMIT’s total assessable income, <a id="s995-1-reduced-by-all-deductions-of-the-amit-net-input-tax-credit"></a>reduced by all deductions of the AMIT. <a id="s995-1-net-input-tax-credit"></a>net input tax credit: Your net input tax credit for an *acquisition <a id="s995-1-or-importation-is"></a>or *importation is:

> <a id="s995-1-1-c"></a>

> <a id="s995-1-1-d"></a>
> **(d)**  the value of a *superannuation interest has the meaning given by section 307-205. > <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>

Water Minister means the Minister administering the Water Act 2007. <a id="s995-1-water-secretary"></a>Water Secretary means the Secretary of the *Water Department. <a id="s995-1-whole-of-life-policy"></a>whole of life policy has the meaning given by section 295-480. wholly-owned group has the meaning given by section 975-500. <a id="s995-1-wholly-owned-subsidiary-of-an-entity-has-the-meaning-given-by-section-703-30-widely-held-company-means"></a><a id="s995-1-wholly-owned-subsidiary-of-an-entity"></a>wholly-owned subsidiary of an entity has the meaning given by section 703-30. <a id="s995-1-widely-held-company-means"></a>widely held company means:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  a company, *shares in which (except shares that carry a right to a fixed rate of *dividend) are listed for quotation in the official list of an *approved stock exchange; or Income Tax Assessment Act 1997                            357 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  a company with more than 50 members, other than a <a id="s995-1-company-where-at-least-one-of-the-following-conditions-is-met-during-an-income-year"></a>company where at least one of the following conditions is <a id="s995-1-met-during-an-income-year"></a>met during an income year:

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-i"></a>
> > **(i)**  no more than 20 persons held, or had the right to acquire or become the holders of, shares representing at least 75% of the value of the shares in the company (other than shares that only carry a right to a fixed rate of dividend);

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-ii"></a>
> > **(ii)**  at least 75% of the voting power in the company was capable of being exercised by no more than 20 persons;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-iii"></a>
> > **(iii)**  at least 75% of the amount of any dividend paid by the company during the year was paid to no more than 20 persons;

> > <a id="s995-1-1-b-iv"></a>
> > **(iv)**  if no dividend was paid by the company during the <a id="s995-1-yearthe-commissioner-is-of-the-opinion-that-if-a-dividend-had-been-paid-by-the-company-during-the-year-at-least-75-of-the-amount-of-the-dividend-would-have-been-paid-to-no-more-than-20-persons-widely-held-entity"></a>year—the Commissioner is of the opinion that, if a dividend had been paid by the company during the year, at least 75% of the amount of the dividend would have been paid to no more than 20 persons. widely held entity has the meaning given by subsection 842-230(2). widely held foreign venture capital fund of funds has the meaning given by subsection 118-420(6). wine has the meaning given by Subdivision 31-A of the *Wine Tax Act. wine tax has the meaning given by section 33-1 of the *Wine Tax Act. Wine Tax Act means the A New Tax System (Wine Equalisation Tax) Act 1999. <a id="s995-1-wine-tax-credit"></a>wine tax credit has the meaning given by section 33-1 of the *Wine Tax Act. wine tax law has the meaning given by section 33-1 of the *Wine Tax Act. 358                                 Income Tax Assessment Act 1997 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 The Dictionary Chapter 6 Dictionary definitions Part 6-5 Definitions Division 995 Section 995-1 <a id="s995-1-wip-amount-asset"></a><a id="s995-1-wip-amount-asset"></a>WIP amount asset has the meaning given by subsection 701-63(6). withholder means a *large withholder, a *medium withholder or a *small withholder. <a id="s995-1-withholding-mit-has-the-meaning-given-by-section-12-383-in-schedule-1-to-the-taxation-administration-act-1953-withholding-payment-means"></a><a id="s995-1-withholding-mit"></a>withholding MIT has the meaning given by section 12-383 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953. withholding payment means:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  a payment from which an amount must be withheld under Division 12 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953 (even if the amount is not withheld); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-aa"></a>
> **(aa)**  a payment that arises because of the operation of section 12A-205 in that Schedule (deemed payments) in respect of which Subdivision 12A-C in that Schedule requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  an *alienated personal services payment in respect of which Division 13 in that Schedule requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-c"></a>
> **(c)**  a *non-cash benefit, the *capital proceeds or a *taxable supply, in respect of which Division 14 in that Schedule requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner.

> **Note:** A withholding payment that consists of a non-cash benefit is made when the benefit is provided. The amount of the withholding payment is taken to be the market value of the benefit at that time.

> **Note:** Divisions 12, 13 and 14 in Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953 deal with collecting amounts on account of income tax payable by the recipient of the payment, alienated personal services payment, non-cash benefit or capital proceeds. withholding payment covered by a particular provision in <a id="s995-1-schedule-1-to-the-taxation-administration-act-1953-means-a-withholding-payment-consisting-of"></a>Schedule 1 to the Taxation Administration Act 1953 means a *withholding payment consisting of:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  a payment from which an amount must be withheld under that provision (even if the amount is not withheld); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-aa"></a>
> **(aa)**  a payment that arises because of the operation of section 12A-205 in that Schedule (deemed payments) in Income Tax Assessment Act 1997                            359 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 respect of which that provision requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  a *non-cash benefit provided by an entity if that provision would have required the entity to withhold an amount if, instead of providing the benefit, the entity had paid the *market value of the benefit; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-c"></a>
> **(c)**  a non-cash benefit provided to an entity if that provision would have required the entity to withhold an amount if the benefit had been a payment of an amount equal to the market value of the benefit; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-d"></a>
> **(d)**  the *capital proceeds in respect of which Subdivision 14-D in that Schedule requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner; or

> <a id="s995-1-1-e"></a>
> **(e)**  the *taxable supply in respect of which Subdivision 14-E in <a id="s995-1-that-schedule-requires-an-amount-to-be-paid-to-the-commissioner-withholding-tax-means-income-tax-payable-under"></a>that Schedule requires an amount to be paid to the Commissioner. withholding tax means income tax payable under:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>
> **(a)**  section 301-175 (departing Australia superannuation payments); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-b"></a>
> **(b)**  section 306-15 (excess untaxed roll-over amounts); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-c"></a>
> **(c)**  Division 840 (withholding taxes); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-d"></a>
> **(d)**  Subdivision 840-M of the Income Tax (Transitional Provisions) Act 1997 (managed investment trust amounts); or

> <a id="s995-1-1-e"></a>
> **(e)**  section 128B of the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936 (dividends, interest and royalties). <a id="s995-1-work-and-income-support-withholding-payments"></a>work and income support withholding payments means work and income support related withholding payments and benefits, within the meaning given by the Income Tax Assessment Act 1936. <a id="s995-1-workers-compensation-law"></a>workers’ compensation law has the meaning given by subsection 136(1) of the Fringe Benefits Tax Assessment Act 1986. <a id="s995-1-work-expense"></a>work expense has the meaning given by section 900-30. <a id="s995-1-working-holiday-maker"></a>working holiday maker has the meaning given by subsection 3A(1) of the Income Tax Rates Act 1986. 360                                 Income Tax Assessment Act 1997 Authorised Version C2026C00122 registered 10/04/2026 The Dictionary Chapter 6 Dictionary definitions Part 6-5 Definitions Division 995 Section 995-1 <a id="s995-1-working-holiday-taxable-income"></a>working holiday taxable income has the meaning given by subsections 3A(2) and (3) of the Income Tax Rates Act 1986. <a id="s995-1-work-in-progress-amount"></a>work in progress amount has the meaning given by section 25-95. worldwide capital amount, for an *outward investing entity (ADI), has the meaning given by section 820-320. worldwide debt of an entity and at a particular time has the meaning given by subsection 820-932(1). <a id="s995-1-worldwide-equity-of-an-entity-and-at-a-particular-time-has-the-meaning-given-by-subsection-820-9322-worldwide-gearing-debt-amount"></a>worldwide equity of an entity and at a particular time has the meaning given by subsection 820-932(2). <a id="s995-1-worldwide-gearing-debt-amount"></a>worldwide gearing debt amount:

> <a id="s995-1-1-a"></a>

> <a id="s995-1-1-c"></a>

> <a id="s995-1-1-e"></a>
